## 📝 Notes

- **Realized P&L:** Actual gains/losses from completed sell transactions
- **Unrealized P&L:** Paper gains/losses from open positions at market close prices
- **Total P&L:** Sum of realized and unrealized P&L
- **Force-Sell:** Handled by `create_smart_orders_from_picks()` when orders expire
- **T+1 Compliance:** Purchases cannot be sold on the same day
- **Status Indicators:**
  - ✅ Normal: Within holding period
  - ⚠️ Expires Next: Will expire next trading day
  - 🔴 Expired: Has exceeded max holding period
